## Finalized Report / Current Recommendation

[UID:0000OY] is the best-supported FILE owner for a standalone NexusTK/login/UserInfoDialogPane.cpp with sibling UserInfoDialogPane.h. The complete source-authored executable inventory is the constructor plus seven ordinary methods at 0x00599cc0, 0x0059b000, 0x0059b0f0, 0x0059b110, 0x0059b130, 0x0059b150, 0x0059b260, and 0x0059b5c0. The two 11-byte adjustor thunks and the scalar deleting destructor are compiler products, not handwritten method bodies. The three-view vtable span and separate 0x00651b54-0x00651bdc RTTI graph are compiler output; the title, validation messages, USERINFO.EPF name, and four option groups are source-authored literals.

The accepted implementation now makes the broad executable page a non-emitting split index. Eight exact source children carry the constructor and ordinary methods. The existing thunk page remains non-emitting, and the deleting wrapper has its own compiler-only child. The read-only aggregate is split into one non-emitting vtable child and one emitting literal/resource child, and the separate 0x00651b54-0x00651bdc RTTI graph has its own non-emitting child. The class page emits a guarded header and the CPP include/root. Controls 0 through 10 are inherited DialogPane child-manager entries, not UserInfoDialogPane pointer members; the only confirmed local storage is wchar_t m_validationMessages[8][128] at +0x26c, producing exact sizeof(UserInfoDialogPane) 0xa6c.

The bounded read-only evidence collected on 2026-08-24 resolves the old constructor-route blocker rather than deferring it. That evidence recorded zero IDA xrefs, zero direct E8/E9/near-conditional transfers, and zero raw VA/RVA/raw-offset pointer hits for 0x00599cc0. The body nevertheless has coherent class-specific vtable stores, exact class-tail initialization, controls, resources, and methods. The strongest conclusion is a retained source-authored but unreachable/dead constructor in this executable, not an unproved indirect factory route and not compiler data. The adjacent UserListDialogPane constructor at 0x0059bc90 has four direct callers and starts a separate social feature.

The dated 2026-08-24 no-loss callback receipt records that B007 changed only the authorized ordinary `by-*` pages plus this report, ran scoped file validators, and physically reread the validator-generated CPP/H outputs. It also records no B007 coverage, IDA, audit/catalog/tracker/registry lifecycle, `execute_report`, archive, or report-movement action during that callback. Ordinary prose does not assert the artifact's current gate, coverage, execution, archive, or IDA state; those states are authoritative only from the artifact's current path plus validator-owned status/history metadata. ## Inference Research Guidance Check

Inference was required for stripped source-facing names, source-file placement, and the source shape of repeated packet/menu operations. It was not used to override direct binary facts.

- Virtual names come from current DialogPane slot authority and matching UserInfo vtable cells.
- Private helper names ValidateInput and SendUserInfo are behavior-based, high-confidence inferred names.
- Control enum names describe exact child indices and behavior; they are not claimed as recovered original spellings.
- The constructor is retained because coherent class-specific source behavior outweighs absent liveness. The liveness absence remains explicit.
- Small static inline helpers in the first draft express repeated inlined conversion/menu logic; they are source-shape guidance, not claims of separate binary functions.
- Korean text is represented with exact ASCII universal-character escapes, preserving UTF-16 values without source-encoding ambiguity.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

The broad-island blank rationale is no longer valid. Exact function boundaries, current support declarations, and complete decompilation make per-method source possible. Splitting removes duplicate-emission risk while preserving every byte.

The old indirect-factory hypothesis was tested through four channels: IDA xrefs, direct relative-transfer scan, little-endian VA/RVA/raw-offset pointer scan, and nearby feature comparison. All UserInfo constructor routes were zero. Positive controls found internal calls 0x0059b058 to 0x0059b260 and 0x0059b066 to 0x0059b5c0, the 0x0062e670 vtable pointer to 0x0059b000, and four direct UserList constructor callers. The zero result is therefore meaningful.

The adjusted callbacks at 0x0059b0f0 and 0x0059b110 physically receive the EventHandler secondary view at complete-object offset +0xa0. Source still declares ordinary UserInfoDialogPane overrides. Their bodies refresh the complete object then base-forward through the secondary interface. This is compiler ABI adjustment, not a separate source class.

UpdateActionButton checks text controls 3 through 6 and popup controls 7 through 9, but not discovery popup 10. ValidateInput checks popup 10 and returns error 7. This asymmetry is preserved: the action button can become enabled before discovery is selected, then submission opens the alert.

ValidateInput does not independently reject empty name/prefix/parent strings or enforce exact resident-ID lengths. Normal UI enablement blocks empty required text, and SetMaxLength caps 8/6/7/8, but the method itself reads suffix character 6 without a minimum-length check. The first draft preserves these facts rather than adding defenses.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- Eleven exact modeled functions partition authored and compiler code.
- Every authored function decompiles and has coherent UserInfo behavior.
- Three class vtable stores occur at 0x00599d20/0x00599d26/0x00599d30.
- Primary slots bind OnControlCommand, UpdateActionButton, and SetHoverControl; secondary slots bind both Event callbacks.
- Eight copied messages fill a 0x800-byte tail after DialogPane size 0x26c.
- The scalar wrapper contains the exact 0xa6c object size.
- Controls are constructed in exact order 0 through 10 and later read by those IDs.
- Sender identity is exactly 0x53 subtype 1 and has one direct source caller.
- UserInfo read-only data has direct constructor refs and exact successor boundary.
- LD01-LD35 plus LA01-LA11 physically partition the complete 0x23c-byte literal child, while external LD36 identifies the pooled `B` dependency without ownership transfer.
- UserList begins at the next aligned function/COL and has independent direct callers.

#ifndef NEXUSTK_LOGIN_USERINFODIALOGPANE_H
#define NEXUSTK_LOGIN_USERINFODIALOGPANE_H

#include "../ui/core/DialogPane.h"

class Event;

enum UserInfoControlId
{
    kUserInfoBackground = 0,
    kUserInfoOkButton = 1,
    kUserInfoCancelButton = 2,
    kUserInfoNameEdit = 3,
    kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixEdit = 4,
    kUserInfoResidentIdSuffixEdit = 5,
    kUserInfoParentNameEdit = 6,
    kUserInfoBloodTypeMenu = 7,
    kUserInfoResidenceMenu = 8,
    kUserInfoPrimaryAccessMethodMenu = 9,
    kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonMenu = 10
};

enum UserInfoValidationResult
{
    kUserInfoNameInvalid = 0,
    kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixInvalid = 1,
    kUserInfoResidentIdSuffixInvalid = 2,
    kUserInfoParentNameInvalid = 3,
    kUserInfoBloodTypeRequired = 4,
    kUserInfoResidenceRequired = 5,
    kUserInfoPrimaryAccessMethodRequired = 6,
    kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonRequired = 7,
    kUserInfoValidationSucceeded = 10
};

class UserInfoDialogPane : public DialogPane
{
public:
    explicit UserInfoDialogPane(const unsigned char *packet);

    virtual bool HandlePointerOrMouseEvent(Event *event);
    virtual bool HandleKeyOrTextEvent(Event *event);
    virtual void OnControlCommand(int controlIndex, int notifyCode);
    virtual void UpdateActionButton();

protected:
    virtual void SetHoverControl(int controlId);

private:
    UserInfoValidationResult ValidateInput();
    void SendUserInfo();
    wchar_t m_validationMessages[8][128];
};

#endif

#include <string.h>

#include "../ui/dialogs/AlertPanes.h"

namespace
{
const wchar_t kUserInfoTitle[] = L"User Information Dialog Pane";
const wchar_t kUserInfoNameError[] =
    L"\uC774\uB984\uC774 \uC798\uBABB \uC785\uB825\uB418\uC5C8\uC2B5\uB2C8\uB2E4.";

const wchar_t kUserInfoBloodTypeError[] =

const wchar_t kUserInfoResidenceError[] =

const wchar_t kUserInfoAccessMethodError[] =
    L"\uC8FC\uB85C\uD558\uB294 \uBC29\uBC95\uC744 \uC785\uB825\uD558\uC2ED\uC2DC\uC694.";
const wchar_t kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonError[] =
    L"\uCC98\uC74C\uD558\uAC8C\uB41C \uB3D9\uAE30\uB97C \uC785\uB825\uD558\uC2ED\uC2DC\uC694.";
const wchar_t kUserInfoResource[] = L"USERINFO.EPF";

static __forceinline void ReadUserInfoField(
    const unsigned char *packet, int *offset, wchar_t *outText)
{
    char encoded[256];
    const unsigned char length = packet[(*offset)++];
    memcpy(encoded, packet + *offset, length);
    *offset += length;
    encoded[length] = 0;
    const int converted = MultiByteToWideChar(
        CP_ACP, 0, encoded, length, outText, 128);
    outText[converted] = 0;
}

static __forceinline void AppendMenuItem(
    PopupMenuControlPane *menu, const wchar_t *text)
{
    menu->AppendItem(new StringMenuItem(text));
}

static __forceinline void SelectMenuText(
    PopupMenuControlPane *menu, const wchar_t *text)
{
    menu->SetSelectedIndex(-1);
    wchar_t itemText[128];
    for (int index = 0; index < menu->GetItemCount(); ++index)
    {
        static_cast<StringMenuItem *>(menu->GetItem(index))->
            CopyText(itemText, 128);
        if (wcscmp(itemText, text) == 0)
        {
            menu->SetSelectedIndex(index);
            break;
        }
    }
}

static __forceinline int AppendUserInfoField(
    unsigned char *packet, int offset, const wchar_t *text)
{
    char encoded[256];
    const int length = WideCharToMultiByte(
        CP_ACP, 0, text, static_cast<unsigned char>(wcslen(text)),
        encoded, 256, NULL, NULL);
    encoded[length] = 0;
    packet[offset++] = static_cast<unsigned char>(length);
    memcpy(packet + offset, encoded, length);
    return offset + length;
}
}

OnControlCommand child RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE, position 20:

~~~cpp
void UserInfoDialogPane::OnControlCommand(
    int controlIndex, int notifyCode)
{
    (void)notifyCode;
    if (controlIndex == kUserInfoOkButton)
    {
        const UserInfoValidationResult result = ValidateInput();
        if (result == kUserInfoValidationSucceeded)
        {
            SendUserInfo();
            SlideCloseVertical();
            CloseDialog();
        }
        else
        {
            new AlertPane(
                m_validationMessages[result], this, L"OK", NULL);
            SetHoverControl(result + kUserInfoNameEdit);
        }
    }
    else if (controlIndex == kUserInfoCancelButton)
    {
        SlideCloseVertical();
        CloseDialog();
    }
}
~~~

void UserInfoDialogPane::UpdateActionButton()
{
    ImageButtonControlPane *okButton =
        GetChild<ImageButtonControlPane>(kUserInfoOkButton);
    const bool canSubmit =
        !GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(kUserInfoNameEdit)->IsEmpty() &&
        !GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(
            kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixEdit)->IsEmpty() &&
        !GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(
            kUserInfoResidentIdSuffixEdit)->IsEmpty() &&
        !GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(
            kUserInfoParentNameEdit)->IsEmpty() &&
        GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoBloodTypeMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() != -1 &&
        GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoResidenceMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() != -1 &&
        GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoPrimaryAccessMethodMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() != -1;
    if (canSubmit) okButton->Enable();
    else okButton->Disable();
}

ValidateInput child RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE, position 70:

~~~cpp
UserInfoValidationResult UserInfoDialogPane::ValidateInput()
{
    wchar_t text[128];
    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(kUserInfoNameEdit)->
        ReadText(text, 128);
    for (unsigned char i = 0;
         i < static_cast<unsigned char>(wcslen(text)); ++i)
        if (text[i] >= L'0' && text[i] <= L'9')
            return kUserInfoNameInvalid;

    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixEdit)->
        ReadText(text, 128);
    unsigned short checksum = 0;
    for (unsigned char i = 0;
         i < static_cast<unsigned char>(wcslen(text)); ++i)
    {
        const unsigned short digit =
            static_cast<unsigned short>(text[i] - L'0');
        if (digit > 9) return kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixInvalid;
        checksum += digit * (i + 2);
    }

    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(kUserInfoResidentIdSuffixEdit)->
        ReadText(text, 128);
    const unsigned char length =
        static_cast<unsigned char>(wcslen(text));
    for (unsigned char i = 0; i < length; ++i)
    {
        const unsigned short digit =
            static_cast<unsigned short>(text[i] - L'0');
        if (digit > 9) return kUserInfoResidentIdSuffixInvalid;
        if (i < length - 1)
            checksum += digit * (i < 2 ? i + 8 : i);
    }
    if (text[6] - L'0' != (11 - checksum % 11) % 10)
        return kUserInfoResidentIdPrefixInvalid;

    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(kUserInfoParentNameEdit)->
        ReadText(text, 128);
    for (unsigned char i = 0;
         i < static_cast<unsigned char>(wcslen(text)); ++i)
        if (text[i] >= L'0' && text[i] <= L'9')
            return kUserInfoParentNameInvalid;

    if (GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoBloodTypeMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() == -1)
        return kUserInfoBloodTypeRequired;
    if (GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoResidenceMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() == -1)
        return kUserInfoResidenceRequired;
    if (GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoPrimaryAccessMethodMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() == -1)
        return kUserInfoPrimaryAccessMethodRequired;
    if (GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(
            kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonMenu)->GetSelectedIndex() == -1)
        return kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonRequired;
    return kUserInfoValidationSucceeded;
}
~~~

SendUserInfo child RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE, position 80:

~~~cpp
void UserInfoDialogPane::SendUserInfo()
{
    unsigned char packet[4096];
    packet[0] = 0x53;
    packet[1] = 1;
    int offset = 4;
    wchar_t text[128];

    for (int id = kUserInfoNameEdit;
         id <= kUserInfoParentNameEdit; ++id)
    {
        GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(id)->ReadText(text, 128);
        offset = AppendUserInfoField(packet, offset, text);
    }
    for (int id = kUserInfoBloodTypeMenu;
         id <= kUserInfoDiscoveryReasonMenu; ++id)
    {
        PopupMenuControlPane *menu =
            GetChild<PopupMenuControlPane>(id);
        static_cast<StringMenuItem *>(
            menu->GetItem(menu->GetSelectedIndex()))->
            CopyText(text, 128);
        offset = AppendUserInfoField(packet, offset, text);
    }

    const unsigned short payloadLength =
        static_cast<unsigned short>(offset - 4);
    packet[2] = static_cast<unsigned char>(payloadLength >> 8);
    packet[3] = static_cast<unsigned char>(payloadLength);
    packet[offset] = 0;
    g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, offset);
}
~~~

The IDA recommendation remains a proposed supervisor-owned handoff, not a completed or gate-passed result. Its sixteen atomic action rows define fail-closed evidence contracts: every A-R stage requires its independently anchored NR baseline/post-dry/post-persistent proofs with enclosing result `kind:"names"`, zero states exact `total:0,data:[]`, and every sole present-name row exactly `{kind:"name",addr,name}`, rejecting missing/wrong nested kind, extra nested members, duplicate/extra/reordered/off-target rows, or wrong address/name; strict dry and persistent summary schemas that preserve request `stop_on_error:true` while accepting response `summary.stop_on_error` only absent-or-true and rejecting false/null/non-boolean/contradictory values; strict `func[]` row schemas with absent-or-empty-string errors, physically absent `dir`/`func_addr`/`new`, no persistent row-level `allow_overwrite`/`stopped`, and no `stopped_at`; and independent readback.
